function loadAdminTestMode(){
  adminTestMode=isAdmin&&localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TEST_MODE_KEY)==="1";
  if(adminTestMode){
    const sandbox=readAdminTestSandbox();
    if(sandbox)applyChallengePayload(sandbox);
    else startFreshAdminTestSandbox();
  }
  renderAdminTestMode();
  document.documentElement.classList.toggle("admin-test-mode",!!adminTestMode);
  syncAdminTestModeFeatures();
  syncSafariShinyTestGate();
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("rf-admin-test-mode-change",{detail:{enabled:!!adminTestMode}}));
}
function renderAdminTestMode(){
  const card=document.getElementById("adminTestModeCard");
  const status=document.getElementById("adminTestModeStatus");
  const btn=document.getElementById("adminTestModeToggleBtn");
  const resetBtn=document.getElementById("adminTestModeResetBtn");
  card?.classList.toggle("hidden",!isAdmin);
  if(status){
    status.textContent=adminTestMode?"Test Mode is ON — you are working in a private test copy. Your real Pokédex/Safari/Shiny progress is protected.":"Test Mode is OFF — you are seeing your real saved challenge progress.";
    status.classList.toggle("on",!!adminTestMode);
  }
  if(btn){
    btn.textContent=adminTestMode?"Turn Test Mode OFF":"Turn Test Mode ON";
    btn.classList.toggle("danger",!!adminTestMode);
    btn.classList.toggle("primary",!adminTestMode);
  }
  resetBtn?.classList.toggle("hidden",!adminTestMode);
}
async function toggleAdminTestMode(){
  if(!isAdmin||!user)return;
  clearTimeout(saveTimer);
  if(!adminTestMode){
    clearAdminTestSandbox();
    startFreshAdminTestSandbox();
    adminTestMode=true;
    localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TEST_MODE_KEY,"1");
    saveAdminTestSandbox();
  }else{
    adminTestMode=false;
    localStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TEST_MODE_KEY);
    clearAdminTestSandbox();
    try{
      const real=await fetchRealChallengePayload();
      applyChallengePayload(real);
    }catch(err){
      console.error(err);
      toast("Could not reload your real challenge save.");
    }
  }
  renderAdminTestMode();
  document.documentElement.classList.toggle("admin-test-mode",!!adminTestMode);
  finishLoad();
  syncAdminTestModeFeatures();
  syncSafariShinyTestGate();
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("rf-admin-test-mode-change",{detail:{enabled:!!adminTestMode}}));
  toast(adminTestMode?"Admin Test Mode enabled — test changes are isolated":"Test Mode disabled — real progress restored");
}
function isAdminTestMode(){return !!(isAdmin&&adminTestMode)}

adminGymAssetsCard?.classList.toggle("hidden",!isAdmin);
adminTeamLeadersCard?.classList.toggle("hidden",!isAdmin);
document.getElementById("adminTeamAvailabilityCard")?.classList.toggle("hidden",!isAdmin);

refreshDisplayNameEditor();
refreshUsernameEditor();

}

async function saveTrainerTeam(next,successMessage){
if(publicMode||!user)return;

if(isAdminTestMode()){
  saveTrainerTestTeamSelections(next);
  renderTrainerTeamEditor();
  closeTrainerTeamPicker();
  toast(successMessage+" Test Mode only.");
  return true;

const seen=new Set();
for(let i=0;i<clean.length;i++){
  const raw=String(clean[i]||""),base=trainerTestSelectionBaseId(raw);
  if(!base)continue;
  if(seen.has(base)){clean[i]="";continue}
  seen.add(base);
  if(trainerTestSelectionIsShiny(raw)&&!trainerUnlockedShinyIds().has(String(base)))clean[i]=base;
}
profile={...(profile||{}),trainer_card_team:clean};renderTrainerCard();renderTrainerTeamEditor();
const{data,error}=await sb.from("profiles").update({trainer_card_team:clean,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()}).eq("id",user.id).select("trainer_card_team").single();
if(error){profile.trainer_card_team=old;renderTrainerCard();renderTrainerTeamEditor();toast("Could not save your Pokémon team.");return false}
profile.trainer_card_team=Array.isArray(data?.trainer_card_team)?data.trainer_card_team:clean;
closeTrainerTeamPicker();toast(successMessage);return true;
}
function chooseTrainerTeamPokemon(selection,pickedPokemon=null){
if(isAdminTestMode()){
  const next=trainerTestTeamSelections(),base=trainerTestSelectionBaseId(selection);
  const existing=next.findIndex(v=>trainerTestSelectionBaseId(v)===base);
  if(existing>=0&&existing!==trainerTeamPickerSlot)return;
  const shiny=trainerTestSelectionIsShiny(selection);
  if(shiny&&!trainerUnlockedShinyIds().has(String(base)))return toast("That shiny is not unlocked.");
  next[trainerTeamPickerSlot]=String(selection);
  const exactPokemon=pickedPokemon||trainerPokemonForChallengeId(base);
  renderTrainerTestPokemonSlotDirect(trainerTeamPickerSlot,exactPokemon,shiny,base);
  saveTrainerTeam(next,shiny?"Shiny Pokémon added to your Trainer Card.":"Trainer Card Pokémon saved.");
  return;
}
const next=trainerLiveTeamSelections(),base=trainerTestSelectionBaseId(selection);
const existing=next.findIndex(v=>trainerTestSelectionBaseId(v)===base);

saveTrainerTeam(next,trainerTestSelectionIsShiny(selection)?"Shiny Pokémon added to your Trainer Card.":"Trainer Card Pokémon saved.");
}

}

adminStatusCard?.classList.toggle("hidden",!isAdmin);
document.getElementById("adminBuildStatusCard")?.classList.toggle("hidden",!isAdmin);

 const localId=document.getElementById("adminBuildLocalId");
 const liveVersion=document.getElementById("adminBuildLiveVersion");
 const liveId=document.getElementById("adminBuildLiveId");
 const badge=document.getElementById("adminBuildLiveBadge");
 if(localVersion)localVersion.textContent=RF_PATCH_NAME;
 if(localId)localId.textContent=`Build ${RF_BUILD}`;
 if(liveVersion)liveVersion.textContent="Press Check Live Version Now when needed.";
 if(liveId)liveId.textContent="No automatic background check.";
 if(badge){badge.textContent="MANUAL";badge.style.background="#e0e7ff";badge.style.color="#3730a3"}
}
document.getElementById("adminMaintenanceCard")?.classList.toggle("hidden",!isAdmin);

async function checkLiveBuildStatus(manual=false){
 const card=document.getElementById("adminBuildStatusCard");
 if(!card||!isAdmin)return;
 const localVersion=document.getElementById("adminBuildLocalVersion");
 const localId=document.getElementById("adminBuildLocalId");
 const liveVersion=document.getElementById("adminBuildLiveVersion");
 const liveId=document.getElementById("adminBuildLiveId");
 const badge=document.getElementById("adminBuildLiveBadge");
 const checked=document.getElementById("adminBuildCheckedAt");
 if(localVersion)localVersion.textContent=RF_PATCH_NAME;
 if(localId)localId.textContent=`Build ${RF_BUILD}`;
 if(manual&&badge)badge.textContent="Checking…";
 try{
  const liveUrl=new URL(location.href);
  liveUrl.hash="";
  liveUrl.searchParams.set("_rfversioncheck",Date.now());
  const html=await fetch(liveUrl.toString(),{cache:"no-store"}).then(r=>{if(!r.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);return r.text()});
  const buildMatch=html.match(/RF_BUILD:(\d+)/)||html.match(/const\s+RF_BUILD=["'](\d+)["']/);
  const patchMatch=html.match(/RF_PATCH_NAME:([^<]+?)\s*-->/);
  const liveBuild=buildMatch?buildMatch[1]:"";
  const livePatch=patchMatch?patchMatch[1].trim():"Unknown patch name";
  if(liveVersion)liveVersion.textContent=livePatch;
  if(liveId)liveId.textContent=liveBuild?`Build ${liveBuild}`:"Build marker not found";
  if(liveBuild===String(RF_BUILD)){
   if(badge){badge.textContent="LIVE ✓";badge.style.background="#dcfce7";badge.style.color="#166534"}
  }else{
   if(badge){badge.textContent="DEPLOYING…";badge.style.background="#fef3c7";badge.style.color="#92400e"}
  }
 }catch(err){
  console.warn("Live build check failed",err);
  if(liveVersion)liveVersion.textContent="Could not check live site";
  if(liveId)liveId.textContent="Try again in a moment.";
  if(badge){badge.textContent="CHECK FAILED";badge.style.background="#fee2e2";badge.style.color="#991b1b"}
 }
 if(checked)checked.textContent=`Last checked ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString([],{hour:"numeric",minute:"2-digit",second:"2-digit"})}`;
}

async function loadGlobalCatalog(){
const rows=[];
const pageSize=1000;
for(let from=0;;from+=pageSize){
const{data,error}=await sb.from("pokemon_catalog")
.select("slug,name,form_name,dex_number,region,category,type_1,type_2,image_url,shiny_image_url,reading_prompt,dex_fact,is_bonus,sort_order,updated_at")
.eq("is_active",true)
.order("sort_order",{ascending:true})
.range(from,from+pageSize-1);
if(error){console.error(error);globalPokemon=[];globalChallengeData=[];return}
const batch=data||[];
rows.push(...batch);
if(batch.length<pageSize)break;
}
globalChallengeData=rows.filter(r=>r.category==="data");
const regularGlobal=rows.filter(r=>r.category!=="data").map(r=>({
id:`global-${r.slug}`,
slug:r.slug,
dex:r.dex_number||"",
name:r.name,
apiName:r.slug,
region:r.region||"Special",
form:r.form_name||"",
isForm:!!r.form_name,
image:r.image_url||"",
shinyImage:r.shiny_image_url||"",
manualImage:!!r.image_url,
manualShinyImage:!!r.shiny_image_url,
catalogSlug:r.slug,
types:[r.type_1,r.type_2].filter(Boolean),
prompt:r.reading_prompt||"",
fact:r.dex_fact||"",
isBonus:!!r.is_bonus,
global:true,
custom:false,
sortOrder:r.sort_order??99999
}));
const simpleRegional=new Set(["alolan","galarian","hisuian","paldean"]);
const skipSyntheticDex=new Set([29,32,669]);
const extraForms=globalChallengeData
.filter(r=>r.form_name&&!simpleRegional.has(String(r.form_name).toLowerCase(

ptions are if you <strong>re-read the book</strong>, or when catching <strong>Unown</strong>. A re-read counts as a new reading, so you may use that new reading to catch a different Pokémon. <strong>For a re-read, BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong> Unown are a special exception: an Unown may reuse a book even if that same reading already counted for any other Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Example:</strong> If you read <em>Alice in Wonderland</em> and use it to catch Bulbasaur, you may still use that same reading to catch Unown A. You still could not use that reading to catch another regular Pokémon.<br><br>For the two special-character forms, <strong>Unown !</strong> requires a book title containing <strong>!</strong>, and <strong>Unown ?</strong> requires a book title containing <strong>?</strong>. Those forms still receive the normal Unown reuse exception.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use the same book again if I re-read it?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Yes.</strong> A re-read counts as a separate reading, so the same book can be used again for another Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Important: BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong><br><br>If both entries do not have dates, or if the dates are the same, the site will treat them as the same reading and will not allow the book to be reused.</div></details><details><summary>What’s the difference between my Display Name and Username?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Display Name</strong> is the name shown around the site, including places like your profile and Trainer Card.<br><br><strong>Username</strong> is your unique account name.<br><br>You can set or update both under <strong>Settings</strong>. You are allowed to change your display name or username when needed, but please avoid changing your username too often so other readers and teammates can still recognize you. Usernames must remain unique and use 3–30 letters, numbers, or underscores.</div></details><details><summary>Is this an official Pokémon website?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>No.</strong> This is an unofficial, noncommercial fan-made reading challenge created by fans for fans. It is not affiliated with, endorsed by, sponsored by, or associated with The Pokémon Company International, Nintendo, Creatures Inc., or GAME FREAK inc.<br><br>Pokémon and all related names, characters, images, and trademarks are the property of their respective owners. No ownership of Pokémon intellectual property is claimed.</div></details><details><summary>Can I add books I read before joining the website?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Yes! Since the website launched in 2026, you can backlog books you read during <strong>2026</strong>, including books finished before creating your account. Each book can still only be used once.</div></details><details><summary>What if I'm not sure my book fits a prompt?</summary><div class="faq-answer">There is <strong>no prompt policing</strong>. If you genuinely think your book fits the prompt, use it. Prompts are here to make choosing books fun, not stressful.</div></details></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>🔎 Pokédex & Catching Pokémon</h3><details><summary>How do I catch a Pokémon?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Find the Pokémon in the Pokédex, complete its reading prompt, add the book you used, and mark it as caught. Your progress saves to your account and contributes to your personal progress and, if applicable, your team's totals.</div></details><details><summary>What does Next Uncaught do?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Next Uncaught moves you to another Pokémon you haven't caught yet, making it easier to browse for your next challenge.</div></details><details><summary>Can I change the book attached to a Pokémon?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Yes. You can update a Pokémon entry if you need to correct or change its book information.</div></details><details><summary>What is “What Others Read”?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Open an individual Pokémon/form and use the <strong>What Others Read</strong> tab inside Books Read to see anonymous book ideas other readers used for that exact Pokémon/form. Only books attached to caught Pokémon are included, duplicate title/author entries are combined, and usernames are never shown.</div></details><details><summary>Do I have to catch Pokémon in a certain order?</summary><div class="faq-answer">No! You can catch Pokémon in <strong>any order you want</strong>. You do not have to follow National Pokédex number order, complete one region before moving to another, or catch an evolution family in sequence. For example, you can catch a final evolution before its earlier stages if that is the Pokémon or prompt you want to complete.</div></details><details><summary>What are the extra Pokédex tags and filters?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Tags and filters help you find special groups of Pokémon, such as Legendary Pokémon, Mythical Pokémon, Starters, Fossils, Trainer Teams, Team Bonuses, Safari encounters, shiny status, and Pokémon types. A tag helps organize Pokémon; it does not automatically mean you earned the matching achievement.</div></details></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>🌿 Safari Zone</h3><details><summary>What is the Safari Zone?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Can't decide what to read next? Select a region — or Any — and the Safari Zone can choose a Pokémon for your next reading challenge. By default, Safari searches your uncaught Pokémon.</div></details><details><summary>Can Safari include Pokémon I've already caught?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Yes. Turn on <strong>All Pokémon — Caught + Uncaught</strong> when you want Safari to include Pokémon you already caught. This is especially useful when shiny hunting. Leave it off when you only want new uncaught Pokémon.</div></details><details><summary>What does Wishlist Pokémon Only do?</summa

}

function drawFractionalStarsCanvas(ctx,rating,x,y,fontSize,fillColor="#f2b01e",baseColor="#aaa"){
 const r=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,Number(rating)||0));
 const stars="★★★★★";

 ctx.restore();
}
function loadCanvasImage(url){return new Promise(resolve=>{if(!url)return resolve(null);const img=new Image();if(!String(url).startsWith("data:"))img.crossOrigin="anonymous";img.onload=()=>resolve(img);img.onerror=()=>resolve(null);img.src=url})}
/* Character art often has a large transparent canvas around the visible figure.
Crop only that transparent padding, then let the existing responsive boxes scale the visible art. */
const visibleCharacterCropCache=new Map();
function visibleCharacterCropUrl(url){
if(!url)return Promise.resolve("");
if(visibleCharacterCropCache.has(url))return visibleCharacterCropCache.get(url);
const job=(async()=>{
try{
const img=await loadCanvasImage(url);if(!img)return url;
const w=img.naturalWidth||img.width,h=img.naturalHeight||img.height;if(!w||!h)return url;
const scan=document.createElement("canvas");scan.width=w;scan.height=h;
const sctx=scan.getContext("2d",{willReadFrequently:true});if(!sctx)return url;
sctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
const data=sctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data;
let minX=w,minY=h,maxX=-1,maxY=-1;
for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
if(data[(y*w+x)*4+3]>8){if(x<minX)minX=x;if(x>maxX)maxX=x;if(y<minY)minY=y;if(y>maxY)maxY=y}
}
if(maxX<minX||maxY<minY)return url;
const bw=maxX-minX+1,bh=maxY-minY+1;
/* Keep a tiny breathing margin so antialiased edges never get clipped. */
const padX=Math.max(2,Math.round(bw*.025)),padY=Math.max(2,Math.round(bh*.025));
const sx=Math.max(0,minX-padX),sy=Math.max(0,minY-padY),ex=Math.min(w,maxX+1+padX),ey=Math.min(h,maxY+1+padY);
const cw=ex-sx,ch=ey-sy;
if(cw>=w*.97&&ch>=h*.97)return url;
const out=document.createElement("canvas");out.width=cw;out.height=ch;
out.getContext("2d").drawImage(img,sx,sy,cw,ch,0,0,cw,ch);
return out.toDataURL("image/png");
}catch(e){console.warn("Visible character fit skipped",e);return url}
})();
visibleCharacterCropCache.set(url,job);return job;
}
async function applyVisibleCharacterFit(img,url){
if(!img||!url)return;
const token=url;img.dataset.visibleFitSource=token;
const fitted=await visibleCharacterCropUrl(url);
if(img.dataset.visibleFitSource!==token)return;
if(fitted&&img.src!==fitted)img.src=fitted;
}
async function loadVisibleCharacterImage(url){const fitted=await visibleCharacterCropUrl(url);return loadCanvasImage(fitted||url)}
function roundedRectPath(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){const rr=Math.min(r,w/2,h/2);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+rr,y);ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,rr);ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,rr);ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,rr);ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,rr);ctx.closePath()}

return false;
}

function factionHasLeader(f){
return f?.is_competitive!==false && String(f?.name||"").toLowerCase()!=="poke dolls";
}
function factionLeaderHtml(f){
if(!factionHasLeader(f))return "";
const character=(f.leader_character||"").trim();
const username=(f.leader_username||"").trim();
if(!username){
const choices=leaderChoicesForFaction(f);

const imageUrl=resolvedLeaderImage({...f,leader_character:name,leader_image_url:""});
const visual=imageUrl?`<img src="${esc(imageUrl)}" alt="${esc(name)}">`:`<div class="team-leader-placeholder">${esc(name[0]||"?")}</div>`;
return `<div class="team-leader-mini">${visual}<div><div class="team-leader-kicker">Team Leader Option</div><div class="team-leader-character">${esc(name)}</div><div class="team-leader-user">Leader spot open</div></div></div>`;
}).join("");
return `<div class="team-leader-choices">${cards}</div>`;
}
}
const imageUrl=resolvedLeaderImage(f);
const visual=imageUrl
?`<img src="${esc(imageUrl)}" alt="${esc(character||"Team leader")}" onerror="this.outerHTML='<div class=&quot;team-leader-placeholder&quot;>?</div>'">`
:`<div class="team-leader-placeholder">${character?esc(character[0].toUpperCase()):"?"}</div>`;
if(!character&&!username&&!imageUrl){
return `<div class="team-leader-open"><strong>Team Leader:</strong> Open</div>`;
}
return `<div class="team-leader-mini">${visual}<div><div class="team-leader-kicker">Team Leader</div><div class="team-leader-character">${esc(character||"Character TBD")}</div><div class="team-leader-user">${username?`@${esc(username)}`:"Leader spot open"}</div></div></div>`;
}

function memberBestName(m){
return (m?.username||m?.display_name||m?.email||"Member").trim();
}
function formatAdminJoinedAt(value){
if(!value)return "Unknown";
const d=new Date(value);
if(!Number.isFinite(d.getTime()))return "Unknown";
return d.toLocaleString([], {year:"numeric",month:"short",day:"numeric",hour:"numeric",minute:"2-digit"});
}

async function loadFactions(){
if(!user||publicMode)return;
const[{data:allFactions,error:fErr},{data:userFaction,error:uErr},{data:memberCounts,error:mcErr}]=await Promise.all([
sb.from("factions").select("*").order("name",{ascending:true}),
sb.from("user_factions").select("faction_id").eq("user_id",user.id).maybeSingle(),
sb.rpc("get_faction_member_counts")
]);
if(fErr){console.error(fErr);factions=[];renderFactions();return}
factions=allFactions||[];
factionMemberCounts={};
if(!mcErr)(memberCounts||[]).forEach(r=>factionMemberCounts[String(r.faction_id)]=Number(r.member_count||0));
joinedFactionId=!uErr&&userFaction?userFaction.faction_id:null;
currentFactionId=joinedFactionId;
renderFactions();
if(joinedFactionId)hideTeamRequiredModal();
else showTeamRequiredModal();
refreshTeamLeaderEditor();
if(document.getElementById("adminUsernameMemberSelect")?.value)loadAdminUsernameEditor(document.getElementById("adminUsernameMemberSelect").value);
applyTeamTheme();
refreshTrainerSettings();
renderTrainerCard();
}
async function chooseFaction(id){
if(!user||publicMode)return;
if(joinedFactionId&&!isAdmin){
toast("Your team choice is locked. Contact Travis or your Team Leader if you need a change.");
return;
}
const f=factions.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));
if(!f)return;
if(!joinedFactionId&&!isAdmin){
const ok=confirm(`Are you sure you want to join ${factionName(f)}?\n\nThis team’s background will become your site theme. Your team choice is locked after joining; if you ever genuinely need to switch, contact Travis or your Team Leader.`);
if(!ok)return;
}
const old=currentFactionId;
previewTrainerSpriteKey="";previewTrainerBannerKey="";
closeTrainerCardEditor();
currentFactionId=id;
renderFactions();
applyTeamTheme();
if(joinedFactionId&&isAdmin){
renderTeamViewMode();
refreshTrainerSettings();
renderTrainerCard();
renderStats();
if(document.getElementById("myteamPage")?.classList.contains("active"))renderMyTeamPage();
toast(`VIEW MODE: ${factionName(f)}. Your account and stats remain ${factionName(joinedFaction())}.`);
return;
}
const{error}=await sb
.from("user_factions")
.upsert(
{user_id:user.id,faction_id:id,selected_at:new Date().toISOString()},
{onConflict:"user_id"}
);
if(error){
console.error(error);
currentFactionId=old;
renderFactions();
if(old)applyTeamTheme();
toast("Could not save team.");
return;
}
joinedFactionId=id;
hideTeamRequiredModal();
renderFactions();
await loadMySpecialBadges();
await loadPendingBadgeNotifications();
refreshTrainerSettings();
renderTrainerCard();
renderHome();
toast(`Joined ${factionName(f)}!`);
if(profile?.faq_orientation_seen!==true)setTimeout(showFaqOrientation,300);
else setTimeout(showNextBadgeEarnedNotification,260);
}

async function migrateLocalData(){try{const old=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const r=indexedDB.open("pokemonReadingChallengeDB",1);r.onerror=()=>reject();r.onsuccess=()=>{const d=r.result;if(!d.objectStoreNames.contains("store"))return resolve(null);const s=d.transaction("store").objectStore("store"),out={},ks=["challengeData","customPokemon","genres"];let n=ks.length;ks.forEach(k=>{const q=s.get(k);q.onsuccess=()=>{out[k]=q.result;if(!--n)resolve(out)};q.onerror=()=>{if(!--n)resolve(out)}})}});if(!old)return toast("No local data found.");challengeData={...challengeData,...(old.challengeData||{})};customPokemon=[...customPokemon,...(old.customPokemon||[]).filter(p=>!customPokemon.some(x=>x.id===p.id))];if(old.genres?.length)genres=old.genres;await saveNow();finishLoad();toast("Local data imported")}catch{toast("Could not import local data.")}}

const LAST_PAGE_KEY="rfLastPageV2",PAGE_HASH_PREFIX="#page-";
function pageFromHash(){
const h=String(location.hash||"");
return h.startsWith(PAGE_HASH_PREFIX)?h.slice(PAGE_HASH_PREFIX.length):"";
}
function savedStartPage(isPublicView=false){
const allowed=isPublicView?["home","dex","stats"]:["home","dex","safari","gyms","myteam","stats","faq","achievements","settings"];
const hashed=pageFromHash();
if(allowed.includes(hashed))return hashed;
let saved="home";
try{saved=localStorage.getItem(LAST_PAGE_KEY)||localStorage.getItem("rfLastPageV1")||"home"}catch{}
return allowed.includes(saved)?saved:"home";
}
function rememberPage(n){
const parent=n==="detail"?"dex":n;
const allowed=publicMode?["home","dex","stats"]:["home","dex","safari","gyms","myteam","stats","faq","achievements","settings"];
if(!allowed.includes(parent))return;
try{localStorage.setItem(LAST_PAGE_KEY,parent)}catch{}
}
function goPage(n,historyMode="push"){
if(requiresTeamSelection()&&!['home','settings'].includes(n)){
 showTeamRequiredModal();
 n='home';
 historyMode='replace';
}
const wasAlreadyActive=document.getElementById(n+"Page")?.classList.contains("active");
document.querySelectorAll(".page").forEach(p=>p.classList.remove("active"));
document.getElementById(n+"Page")?.classList.add("active");
document.querySelectorAll(".nav-btn").forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle("active",b.dataset.page===n));
document.body.dataset.page=n;
syncTeamPageBackground(n);
if(n!=="detail"){
rememberPage(n);
const allowed=publicMode?["home","dex","stats"]:["home","dex","safari","gyms","myteam","stats","faq","achievements","settings"];
if(allowed.includes(n)&&historyMode!=="none"){
const nextHash=PAGE_HASH_PREFIX+n;
const url=location.pathname+location.search+nextHash;
try{
if(historyMode==="replace")history.replaceState({rfPage:n},"",url);
else if(location.hash!==nextHash)history.pushState({rfPage:n},"",url);
else history.replaceState({rfPage:n},"",url);
}catch{
if(location.hash!==nextHash)location.hash=nextHash;
}
}
}
if(n==="home")renderHome();
if(n==="dex"&&!wasAlreadyActive)renderDex();
if(n==="safari")renderSafari();
if(n==="gyms"){if(gymView==="badges")renderBadgeCase();else renderGyms();}
if(n==="myteam")renderMyTeamPage();
if(n==="stats")renderStats();
if(n==="faq")renderSiteUpdates();
if(n==="achievements")renderAchievementShell();
if(n==="settings")renderFactions();
scrollTo(0,0)
}

function getSiteTheme(){
try{return localStorage.getItem(RF_SITE_THEME_KEY)==="dark"?"dark":"light"}catch{return document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme==="dark"?"dark":"light"}
}
function refreshSiteThemeControls(){
const mode=getSiteTheme();
document.getElementById("siteThemeLightBtn")?.classList.toggle("active",mode==="light");
document.getElementById("siteThemeDarkBtn")?.classList.toggle("active",mode==="dark");
document.getElementById("siteThemeLightBtn")?.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(mode==="light"));
document.getElementById("siteThemeDarkBtn")?.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(mode==="dark"));
}
function setSiteTheme(mode){
const next=mode==="dark"?"dark":"light";
try{localStorage.setItem(RF_SITE_THEME_KEY,next)}catch{}
if(next==="dark")document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme="dark";else delete document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme;
refreshSiteThemeControls();
toast(`${next==="dark"?"Dark":"Light"} mode enabled.`);
}

});

});
}

function getSiteTheme(){
try{return localStorage.getItem(RF_SITE_THEME_KEY)==="dark"?"dark":"light"}catch{return document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme==="dark"?"dark":"light"}
}
function refreshSiteThemeControls(){
const mode=getSiteTheme();
document.getElementById("siteThemeLightBtn")?.classList.toggle("active",mode==="light");
document.getElementById("siteThemeDarkBtn")?.classList.toggle("active",mode==="dark");
document.getElementById("siteThemeLightBtn")?.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(mode==="light"));
document.getElementById("siteThemeDarkBtn")?.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(mode==="dark"));
}
function setSiteTheme(mode){
const next=mode==="dark"?"dark":"light";
try{localStorage.setItem(RF_SITE_THEME_KEY,next)}catch{}
if(next==="dark")document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme="dark";else delete document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme;
refreshSiteThemeControls();
toast(`${next==="dark"?"Dark":"Light"} mode enabled.`);
}
function initSettingsSections(){
document.querySelectorAll("#settingsPage > .form-card").forEach(card=>{
const heading=card.querySelector(":scope > h3, :scope > .admin-tools-title");
if(!heading||heading.dataset.collapseReady)return;
card.classList.add("settings-collapsible");heading.dataset.collapseReady="1";heading.classList.add("settings-section-heading");heading.tabIndex=0;heading.setAttribute("role","button");heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
const toggle=()=>{const collapsed=card.classList.toggle("settings-collapsed");heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(!collapsed))};
heading.addEventListener("click",toggle);heading.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"||e.key===" "){e.preventDefault();toggle()}});
});
}

}

openPokemon(target.id,true);
return;
}
goPage(allowed.includes(target.page)?target.page:"home","none");
});
function getSiteTheme(){
try{return localStorage.getItem(RF_SITE_THEME_KEY)==="dark"?"dark":"light"}catch{return document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme==="dark"?"dark":"light"}
}
function refreshSiteThemeControls(){
const mode=getSiteTheme();
document.getElementById("siteThemeLightBtn")?.classList.toggle("active",mode==="light");
document.getElementById("siteThemeDarkBtn")?.classList.toggle("active",mode==="dark");
document.getElementById("siteThemeLightBtn")?.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(mode==="light"));
document.getElementById("siteThemeDarkBtn")?.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(mode==="dark"));
}
function setSiteTheme(mode){
const next=mode==="dark"?"dark":"light";
try{localStorage.setItem(RF_SITE_THEME_KEY,next)}catch{}
if(next==="dark")document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme="dark";else delete document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme;
refreshSiteThemeControls();
toast(`${next==="dark"?"Dark":"Light"} mode enabled.`);
}
function initSettingsSections(){
document.querySelectorAll("#settingsPage > .form-card").forEach(card=>{
const heading=card.querySelector(":scope > h3, :scope > .admin-tools-title");
if(!heading||heading.dataset.collapseReady)return;
card.classList.add("settings-collapsible");heading.dataset.collapseReady="1";heading.classList.add("settings-section-heading");heading.tabIndex=0;heading.setAttribute("role","button");heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
const toggle=()=>{const collapsed=card.classList.toggle("settings-collapsed");heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(!collapsed))};
heading.addEventListener("click",toggle);heading.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"||e.key===" "){e.preventDefault();toggle()}});
});
}
function setAllSettingsCollapsed(collapsed){
initSettingsSections();
document.querySelectorAll("#settingsPage > .form-card.settings-collapsible, #settingsPage > .form-card").forEach(card=>{const heading=card.querySelector(":scope > .settings-section-heading");if(!heading)return;card.classList.toggle("settings-collapsed",collapsed);heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(!collapsed))});
}
function updateBackToTop(){document.getElementById("backToTopBtn")?.classList.toggle("visible",window.scrollY>500)}
window.addEventListener("scroll",updateBackToTop,{passive:true});
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",()=>{initSettingsSections();refreshSiteThemeControls();updateBackToTop();loadSiteMaintenance();setInterval(loadSiteMaintenance,60000)});
async function init(){
const u=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("u");
if(u)return startPublic(u);
const recoveryLink=isPasswordRecoveryUrl();
const{data:{session}}=await sb.auth.getSession();
user=session?.user||null;
if(user){await startPrivate();if(recoveryLink)showPasswordRecovery()}else{document.getElementById("loginLoadOverlay")?.classList.add("hidden");authPage.classList.add("active");}
sb.auth.onAuthStateChange((e,s)=>{
if(e==="PASSWORD_RECOVERY"){user=s?.user||user;setTimeout(()=>showPasswordRecovery(),0);return}
if(e==="SIGNED_IN"&&!user){user=s.user;setTimeout(()=>startPrivate(),0)}
});
}
document.getElementById("gymLeaderImage")?.addEventListener("input",renderGymAssetPreviews);
document.getElementById("gymBadgeImage")?.addEventListener("input",renderGymAssetPreviews);
document.getElementById("teamLeaderCharacter")?.addEventListener("input",renderTeamLeaderPreview);
document.getElementById("teamLeaderUsername")?.addEventListener("input",renderTeamLeaderPreview);
document.getElementById("teamLeaderImage")?.addEventListener("input",renderTeamLeaderPreview);
setInterval(refreshCloudTime,15000);
init();
document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
if(e.key==="Escape"&&!document.getElementById("teamThemePreviewModal")?.classList.contains("hidden"))closeTeamThemePreview();
});
document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
if(e.key==="Escape"&&!document.getElementById("catchCardModal")?.classList.contains("hidden"))closeCatchCard();
});
</script>

}

function updatePokemonPromptToggles(){
  (dexGrid?.querySelectorAll(".pokemon-card-prompt-wrap")||[]).forEach(wrap=>{
    const prompt=wrap.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt");
    const btn=wrap.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt-toggle");
    if(!prompt||!btn)return;
    const expanded=prompt.classList.contains("expanded");
    if(expanded){
      btn.classList.remove("hidden");
      btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
      btn.textContent="⌃";
      btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Collapse reading prompt");
      return;
    }
    const overflowing=prompt.scrollHeight>prompt.clientHeight+2;
    btn.classList.toggle("hidden",!overflowing);
    btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
    btn.textContent="⌄";
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Expand reading prompt");
  });
}
function togglePokemonCardPrompt(btn){
  const wrap=btn?.closest(".pokemon-card-prompt-wrap");
  const prompt=wrap?.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt");
  if(!prompt)return;
  const expanded=prompt.classList.toggle("expanded");
  btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded",expanded?"true":"false");
  btn.textContent=expanded?"⌃":"⌄";
  btn.setAttribute("aria-label",expanded?"Collapse reading prompt":"Expand reading prompt");
}

function promptPdfSafeText(v){
  return String(v??"")
    .replace(/[–—]/g,"-")
    .replace(/[“”]/g,'"')
    .replace(/[‘’]/g,"'")
    .replace(/\u00a0/g," ")
    .replace(/\s+/g," ")
    .trim();
}
function promptPdfPokemonLabel(p){
  const num=p?.dex?`#${String(p.dex).padStart(4,"0")} `:"";
  const form=p?.form?` - ${p.form}`:"";
  return `${num}${p?.name||"Pokémon"}${form}`;
}
function promptPdfPromptFor(p){
  const e=challengeData[p.id]||{};
  return p.custom?(e.prompt||p.prompt||""):(p.prompt||e.prompt||"");
}
function promptPdfBooksFor(p){
  const e=challengeData[p.id]||{};
  return Array.isArray(e.books)?e.books.filter(Boolean):[];
}
function promptPdfSortedPokemon(){
  return [...all()].sort((a,b)=>{
    const d=(+a.dex||99999)-(+b.dex||99999);
    if(d)return d;
    if(+a.dex===201&&+b.dex===201)return String(a.form||"").localeCompare(String(b.form||""),undefined,{numeric:true});
    const sa=a.sortOrder??99999,sb=b.sortOrder??99999;
    if(sa!==sb)return sa-sb;
    const forms=Number(!!a.isForm)-Number(!!b.isForm);
    if(forms)return forms;
    return String(a.form||a.name||"").localeCompare(String(b.form||b.name||""),undefined,{numeric:true});
  });
}

  if(publicMode)return toast("Prompt List downloads are available from your signed-in Reading Dex.");

setupDexInfiniteLoader();
schedulePokemonPromptToggleScan();
}
function updatePokemonPromptToggles(){
  (dexGrid?.querySelectorAll(".pokemon-card-prompt-wrap")||[]).forEach(wrap=>{
    const prompt=wrap.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt");
    const btn=wrap.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt-toggle");
    if(!prompt||!btn)return;
    const expanded=prompt.classList.contains("expanded");
    if(expanded){
      btn.classList.remove("hidden");
      btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
      btn.textContent="⌃";
      btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Collapse reading prompt");
      return;
    }
    const overflowing=prompt.scrollHeight>prompt.clientHeight+2;
    btn.classList.toggle("hidden",!overflowing);
    btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
    btn.textContent="⌄";
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Expand reading prompt");
  });
}
function togglePokemonCardPrompt(btn){
  const wrap=btn?.closest(".pokemon-card-prompt-wrap");
  const prompt=wrap?.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt");
  if(!prompt)return;
  const expanded=prompt.classList.toggle("expanded");
  btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded",expanded?"true":"false");
  btn.textContent=expanded?"⌃":"⌄";
  btn.setAttribute("aria-label",expanded?"Collapse reading prompt":"Expand reading prompt");
}

function promptPdfSafeText(v){
  return String(v??"")
    .replace(/[–—]/g,"-")
    .replace(/[“”]/g,'"')
    .replace(/[‘’]/g,"'")
    .replace(/\u00a0/g," ")
    .replace(/\s+/g," ")
    .trim();
}
function promptPdfPokemonLabel(p){
  const num=p?.dex?`#${String(p.dex).padStart(4,"0")} `:"";
  const form=p?.form?` - ${p.form}`:"";
  return `${num}${p?.name||"Pokémon"}${form}`;
}
function promptPdfPromptFor(p){
  const e=challengeData[p.id]||{};
  return p.custom?(e.prompt||p.prompt||""):(p.prompt||e.prompt||"");
}
function promptPdfBooksFor(p){
  const e=challengeData[p.id]||{};
  return Array.isArray(e.books)?e.books.filter(Boolean):[];
}
function promptPdfSortedPokemon(){
  return [...all()].sort((a,b)=>{
    const d=(+a.dex||99999)-(+b.dex||99999);
    if(d)return d;
    if(+a.dex===201&&+b.dex===201)return String(a.form||"").localeCompare(String(b.form||""),undefined,{numeric:true});
    const sa=a.sortOrder??99999,sb=b.sortOrder??99999;
    if(sa!==sb)return sa-sb;
    const forms=Number(!!a.isForm)-Number(!!b.isForm);
    if(forms)return forms;
    return String(a.form||a.name||"").localeCompare(String(b.form||b.name||""),undefined,{numeric:true});
  });
}
function downloadPromptListPdf(){
  if(publicMode)return toast("Prompt List downloads are available from your signed-in Reading Dex.");
  const jsPDF=window.jspdf?.jsPDF;
  if(!jsPDF){
    toast("PDF tools are still loading. Try again in a moment.");
    return;
  }

  const mons=promptPdfSortedPokemon();
  if(!mons.length){
    toast("Pokédex data is still loading.");
    return;
  }

  const doc=new jsPDF({unit:"pt",format:"letter",orientation:"portrait"});
  const pageW=doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth();
  const pageH=doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight();
  const left=44,right=44,top=48,bottom=42;
  const contentW=pageW-left-right;
  let y=top;
  let pageNo=1;

  const username=promptPdfSafeText(profile?.username||profile?.display_name||"Reader");
  const generated=new Date().toLocaleDateString(undefined,{year:"numeric",month:"long",day:"numeric"});

  function footer(){
    doc.setDrawColor(210);
    doc.line(left,pageH-30,pageW-right,pageH-30);
    doc.setFont("helvetica","normal");
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.setTextColor(110);
    doc.text(`Reading Frenzy - Pokémon Reading Challenge  |  ${username}  |  Page ${pageNo}`,left,pageH-17);
  }
  function newPage(){
    footer();
    doc.addPage();
    pageNo++;
    y=top;
  }
  function ensure(space){
    if(y+space>pageH-bottom)newPage();
  }
  function wrapped(textValue,maxWidth,fontSize=10,fontStyle="normal"){
    doc.setFont("helvetica",fontStyle);
    doc.setFontSize(fontSize);
    return doc.splitTextToSize(promptPdfSafeText(textValue),maxWidth);
  }
  function writeLines(lines,x,lineH){
    for(const line of lines){
      ensure(lineH+2);
      doc.text(line,x,y);
      y+=lineH;
    }
  }

  // Title page heading.
  doc.setTextColor(30);
  doc.setFont("helvetica","bold");
  doc.setFontSize(20);
  doc.text("Reading Frenzy - Pokémon Prompt List",left,y);
  y+=24;
  doc.setFont("helvetica","normal");
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.setTextColor(90);
  doc.text(`Generated ${promptPdfSafeText(generated)} for ${username}`,left,y);
  y+=22;
  doc.setFontSize(9);
  doc.text("Each entry include

===== TERM 'expanded' AT 26384497 =====
type-row">${types}</div>`:""}${monthlyBonusForPokemon(p)?`<span class="monthly-bonus-chip">⭐ MONTHLY BONUS • ${Number(monthlyBonusForPokemon(p).multiplier||2)}×</span>`:""}${achievementTagsHtml(p)}${currentlyCatching(p)?`<span class="catching-chip">🎯 Currently Catching</span>`:""}${cardEntry.plannedBook&&!publicMode?`<span class="planned-chip" title="${esc(cardEntry.plannedBook)}">📘 ${esc(cardEntry.plannedBook)}</span>`:""}<div class="pokemon-card-prompt-wrap"><div class="pokemon-card-prompt"><span>Prompt</span><div class="pokemon-card-prompt-text">${esc(cardPrompt||"Prompt coming soon.")}</div></div><button class="pokemon-card-prompt-toggle hidden" type="button" aria-label="Expand reading prompt" aria-expanded="false" onclick="event.stopPropagation();togglePokemonCardPrompt(this)">⌄</button></div>${editTools}`;
if(!editable)b.onclick=()=>openPokemon(p.id);
dexFragment.appendChild(b)
})
if(dexBatchShown<dexCurrentFiltered.length){
  const sentinel=document.createElement("div");
  sentinel.id="dexLoadSentinel";
  sentinel.className="empty";
  sentinel.style.cursor="pointer";
  sentinel.textContent=`Loading more Pokémon… (${Math.min(dexBatchShown,dexCurrentFiltered.length)} of ${dexCurrentFiltered.length})`;
  dexFragment.appendChild(sentinel);
}
if(preserveBatch)dexGrid.appendChild(dexFragment);
else dexGrid.replaceChildren(dexFragment);
prioritizeVisibleDexImages();
setupAheadOfScrollPreload();
setupDexInfiniteLoader();
schedulePokemonPromptToggleScan();
}

function updatePokemonPromptToggles(){
  (dexGrid?.querySelectorAll(".pokemon-card-prompt-wrap")||[]).forEach(wrap=>{
    const prompt=wrap.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt");
    const btn=wrap.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt-toggle");
    if(!prompt||!btn)return;
    const expanded=prompt.classList.contains("expanded");
    if(expanded){
      btn.classList.remove("hidden");
      btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
      btn.textContent="⌃";
      btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Collapse reading prompt");
      return;
    }
    const overflowing=prompt.scrollHeight>prompt.clientHeight+2;
    btn.classList.toggle("hidden",!overflowing);
    btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
    btn.textContent="⌄";
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Expand reading prompt");
  });
}
function togglePokemonCardPrompt(btn){
  const wrap=btn?.closest(".pokemon-card-prompt-wrap");
  const prompt=wrap?.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt");
  if(!prompt)return;
  const expanded=prompt.classList.toggle("expanded");
  btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded",expanded?"true":"false");
  btn.textContent=expanded?"⌃":"⌄";
  btn.setAttribute("aria-label",expanded?"Collapse reading prompt":"Expand reading prompt");
}

function promptPdfSafeText(v){
  return String(v??"")
    .replace(/[–—]/g,"-")
    .replace(/[“”]/g,'"')
    .replace(/[‘’]/g,"'")
    .replace(/\u00a0/g," ")
    .replace(/\s+/g," ")
    .trim();
}
function promptPdfPokemonLabel(p){
  const num=p?.dex?`#${String(p.dex).padStart(4,"0")} `:"";
  const form=p?.form?` - ${p.form}`:"";
  return `${num}${p?.name||"Pokémon"}${form}`;
}
function promptPdfPromptFor(p){
  const e=challengeData[p.id]||{};
  return p.custom?(e.prompt||p.prompt||""):(p.prompt||e.prompt||"");
}
function promptPdfBooksFor(p){
  const e=challengeData[p.id]||{};
  return Array.isArray(e.books)?e.books.filter(Boolean):[];
}
function promptPdfSortedPokemon(){
  return [...all()].sort((a,b)=>{
    const d=(+a.dex||99999)-(+b.dex||99999);
    if(d)return d;
    if(+a.dex===201&&+b.dex===201)return String(a.form||"").localeCompare(String(b.form||""),undefined,{numeric:true});
    const sa=a.sortOrder??99999,sb=b.sortOrder??99999;
    if(sa!==sb)return sa-sb;
    const forms=Number(!!a.isForm)-Number(!!b.isForm);
    if(forms)return forms;
    return String(a.form||a.name||"").localeCompare(String(b.form||b.name||""),undefined,{numeric:true});
  });
}
function downloadPromptListPdf(){
  if(publicMode)return toast("Prompt List downloads are available from your signed-in Reading Dex.");
  const jsPDF=window.jspdf?.jsPDF;
  if(!jsPDF){
    toast("PDF tools are still loading. Try again in a moment.");

  if(!mons.length){
    toast("Pokédex data is still loading.");
    return;

  const doc=new jsPDF({unit:"pt",format:"letter",orientation:"portrait"});
  const pageW=doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth();
  const pageH=doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight();
  const left=44,right=44,top=48,bottom=42;
  const contentW=pageW-left-right;

    doc.setFontSize(fontSize);
    return doc.splitTextToSize(promptPdfSafeText(textValue),maxWidth);
  }
  function writeLines(lines,x,lineH){
    for(const line of lines){
      ensure(lineH+2);
      doc.text(line,x,y);
      y+=lineH;
    }
  }

  // Title page heading.
  doc.setTextColor(30);
  doc.setFont("helvetica","bold");
  doc.setFontSize(20);
  doc.text("Reading Frenzy - Pokémon Prompt List",left,y);
  y+=24;
  doc.setFont("helvetica","normal");

  y+=26;

function updatePokemonPromptToggles(){
  (dexGrid?.querySelectorAll(".pokemon-card-prompt-wrap")||[]).forEach(wrap=>{
    const prompt=wrap.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt");
    const btn=wrap.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt-toggle");
    if(!prompt||!btn)return;
    const expanded=prompt.classList.contains("expanded");
    if(expanded){
      btn.classList.remove("hidden");
      btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
      btn.textContent="⌃";
      btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Collapse reading prompt");
      return;
    }
    const overflowing=prompt.scrollHeight>prompt.clientHeight+2;
    btn.classList.toggle("hidden",!overflowing);
    btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded","false");
    btn.textContent="⌄";
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Expand reading prompt");
  });
}
function togglePokemonCardPrompt(btn){
  const wrap=btn?.closest(".pokemon-card-prompt-wrap");
  const prompt=wrap?.querySelector(".pokemon-card-prompt");
  if(!prompt)return;
  const expanded=prompt.classList.toggle("expanded");
  btn.setAttribute("aria-expanded",expanded?"true":"false");
  btn.textContent=expanded?"⌃":"⌄";
  btn.setAttribute("aria-label",expanded?"Collapse reading prompt":"Expand reading prompt");
}

function promptPdfSafeText(v){
  return String(v??"")
    .replace(/[–—]/g,"-")
    .replace(/[“”]/g,'"')
    .replace(/[‘’]/g,"'")
    .replace(/\u00a0/g," ")
    .replace(/\s+/g," ")
    .trim();
}
function promptPdfPokemonLabel(p){
  const num=p?.dex?`#${String(p.dex).padStart(4,"0")} `:"";
  const form=p?.form?` - ${p.form}`:"";
  return `${num}${p?.name||"Pokémon"}${form}`;
}

  const e=challengeData[p.id]||{};
  return p.custom?(e.prompt||p.prompt||""):(p.prompt||e.prompt||"");

  const e=challengeData[p.id]||{};
  return Array.isArray(e.books)?e.books.filter(Boolean):[];
}

    return String(a.form||a.name||"").localeCompare(String(b.form||b.name||""),undefined,{numeric:true});
  });

async function setDetailBooksTab(tab){
 if(publicMode)return;
 detailBooksTab=tab==="community"?"community":"mine";
 communityBooksExpanded=false;
 renderBooks();
 if(detailBooksTab==="community"&&communityBooksPokemonId!==String(currentPokemonId||""))await loadCommunityBooks(currentPokemonId);
}
async function loadCommunityBooks(pokemonId){
 const key=String(pokemonId||"");if(!key)return;
 communityBooksLoading=true;communityBooks=[];communityBooksPokemonId=key;renderBooks();
 try{
  const{data,error}=await sb.rpc("get_community_books",{p_pokemon_key:key});
  if(error)throw error;
  if(String(currentPokemonId||"")!==key)return;
  communityBooks=Array.isArray(data)?data:[];
 }catch(e){console.warn("Could not load community books",e);communityBooks=[]}
 finally{if(String(currentPokemonId||"")===key){communityBooksLoading=false;renderBooks()}}
}
function toggleCommunityBooksExpanded(){communityBooksExpanded=!communityBooksExpanded;renderBooks()}
function renderCommunityBooks(){
 const list=document.getElementById("bookList");if(!list)return;
 if(communityBooksLoading){list.innerHTML='<div class="muted">Loading books other readers used…</div>';return}
 const rows=Array.isArray(communityBooks)?communityBooks:[];
 if(!rows.length){list.innerHTML='<div class="muted">No community books yet for this exact Pokémon/form.</div>';return}
 const visible=communityBooksExpanded?rows:rows.slice(0,5);
 list.innerHTML=`<div class="dex43-community-summary">${rows.length} unique book${rows.length===1?"":"s"} used by other readers for this exact Pokémon/form.</div><div class="dex43-community-list ${communityBooksExpanded?"expanded":""}">${visible.map(b=>`<div class="dex43-community-book"><strong>${esc(b.title||"Untitled")}</strong>${b.author?`<div class="muted">${esc(b.author)}</div>`:""}</div>`).join("")}</div>${rows.length>5?`<button class="secondary dex43-community-more" type="button" onclick="toggleCommunityBooksExpanded()">${communityBooksExpanded?"Show Less":"View More"}</button>`:""}`;
}
function formatBookDate(v){const d=new Date(String(v)+"T00:00:00");return isNaN(d)?String(v):d.toLocaleDateString(undefined,{year:"numeric",month:"short",day:"numeric"})}
function renderBooks(){
const e=entry(currentPokemonId);
const books=Array.isArray(e.books)?e.books:[];
const list=document.getElementById("bookList");
if(!list)return;
const mineTab=document.getElementById("detailMyBooksTab"),othersTab=document.getElementById("detailOthersBooksTab"),addBtn=document.getElementById("detailAddBookBtn");
if(mineTab)mineTab.classList.toggle("active",detailBooksTab!=="community");
if(othersTab)othersTab.classList.toggle("active",detailBooksTab==="community");
if(addBtn)addBtn.classList.toggle("hidden",detailBooksTab==="community");
if(!publicMode&&detailBooksTab==="community"){renderCommunityBooks();return}
list.innerHTML=books.length?"":'<div class="muted">No books added yet. Add the title you read to unlock “Mark as Caught.”</div>';
books.forEach((raw,i)=>{
const b=(raw&&typeof raw==="object")?raw:{};
list.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend",`<div class="book-row ${b.cover?"has-cover":"no-cover"}">${b.cover?`<img class="book-cover" src="${esc(b.cover)}">`:""}<div><strong>${esc(b.title||"Untitled")}</strong>${b.author?`<div class="muted">${esc(b.author)}</div>`:""}${b.dateRead?`<div class="muted">Read ${esc(formatBookDate(b.dateRead))}</div>`:""}${bookRatingHtml(i,b)}${publicMode?"":`<div class="book-actions">${e.caught?`<button class="small-btn catch-card-book-btn" onclick="openCatchCard(${i})">✨ Catch Card</button>`:""}<button class="small-btn" onclick="bookModal(${i})">Edit</button><button class="small-btn" onclick="deleteBook(${i})">Delete</button></div>`}</div></div>`)
});
if(!publicMode&&e.caught&&books.length>=2){
list.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend",`<div class="catch-card-all-wrap"><button class="secondary catch-card-all-btn" type="button" onclick="openAllBooksCatchCard()">✨ Catch Card — All Books</button></div>`);
}
}

function catchCardFirstType(p){
return String(p?.types?.[0]||"normal").toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z]/g,"")||"normal";
}
function catchCardDisplayName(p){
return `${p?.name||"Pokémon"}${p?.form?` • ${p.form}`:""}`;
}
function catchCardRoundedRect(ctx,x,y,w,h,r){
const radius=Math.min(r,w/2,h/2);
ctx.beginPath();
ctx.moveTo(x+radius,y);
ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,radius);
ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,radius);
ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,radius);
ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,radius);
ctx.clos

function getSiteTheme(){
try{return localStorage.getItem(RF_SITE_THEME_KEY)==="dark"?"dark":"light"}catch{return document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme==="dark"?"dark":"light"}
}
function refreshSiteThemeControls(){
const mode=getSiteTheme();
document.getElementById("siteThemeLightBtn")?.classList.toggle("active",mode==="light");
document.getElementById("siteThemeDarkBtn")?.classList.toggle("active",mode==="dark");
document.getElementById("siteThemeLightBtn")?.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(mode==="light"));
document.getElementById("siteThemeDarkBtn")?.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(mode==="dark"));
}
function setSiteTheme(mode){
const next=mode==="dark"?"dark":"light";
try{localStorage.setItem(RF_SITE_THEME_KEY,next)}catch{}
if(next==="dark")document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme="dark";else delete document.documentElement.dataset.siteTheme;
refreshSiteThemeControls();
toast(`${next==="dark"?"Dark":"Light"} mode enabled.`);
}
function initSettingsSections(){
document.querySelectorAll("#settingsPage > .form-card").forEach(card=>{
const heading=card.querySelector(":scope > h3, :scope > .admin-tools-title");
if(!heading||heading.dataset.collapseReady)return;
card.classList.add("settings-collapsible");heading.dataset.collapseReady="1";heading.classList.add("settings-section-heading");heading.tabIndex=0;heading.setAttribute("role","button");heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded","true");
const toggle=()=>{const collapsed=card.classList.toggle("settings-collapsed");heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(!collapsed))};
heading.addEventListener("click",toggle);heading.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"||e.key===" "){e.preventDefault();toggle()}});
});
}
function setAllSettingsCollapsed(collapsed){
initSettingsSections();
document.querySelectorAll("#settingsPage > .form-card.settings-collapsible, #settingsPage > .form-card").forEach(card=>{const heading=card.querySelector(":scope > .settings-section-heading");if(!heading)return;card.classList.toggle("settings-collapsed",collapsed);heading.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(!collapsed))});
}

sb.auth.onAuthStateChange((e,s)=>{
if(e==="PASSWORD_RECOVERY"){user=s?.user||user;setTimeout(()=>showPasswordRecovery(),0);return}
if(e==="SIGNED_IN"&&!user){user=s.user;setTimeout(()=>startPrivate(),0)}
});
}

<section id="faqPage" class="page"><div class="faq-shell"><div class="faq-hero"><div class="faq-kicker">READING FRENZY</div><h2>Pokémon Reading Challenge FAQ</h2><p>Everything you need to know about catching Pokémon, Safari and shiny hunting, achievements, Gym Battles, teams, Trainer Cards, backups, and the community.</p><div class="faq-evergreen"><strong>No deadline!</strong> The Pokémon Reading Challenge is ongoing and has no end date. It has been running for about nine years — the website is just the newest way to participate.</div></div><div id="recentUpdatesSection" class="faq-section recent-updates-section"><div class="recent-updates-heading"><div><div class="faq-kicker">WHAT'S NEW</div><h3>📣 Recent Updates</h3></div><button id="addSiteUpdateBtn" class="primary hidden" type="button" onclick="openSiteUpdateEditor()">+ Add Update</button></div><div id="siteUpdateEditor" class="site-update-editor hidden"><input id="siteUpdateTitle" maxlength="120" placeholder="Update title"><label for="siteUpdateDate">Date</label><input id="siteUpdateDate" type="date"><label for="siteUpdateBody">Description</label><textarea id="siteUpdateBody" maxlength="2000" rows="5" placeholder="Explain what changed..."></textarea><div class="site-update-editor-actions"><button class="primary" type="button" onclick="saveSiteUpdate()">Save Update</button><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="cancelSiteUpdateEditor()">Cancel</button></div></div><div id="siteUpdatesList" class="site-updates-list"><div class="muted">Loading recent updates…</div></div><button id="siteUpdatesViewMoreBtn" class="secondary site-updates-view-more hidden" type="button" onclick="toggleAllSiteUpdates()">View More Updates</button></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>📖 Getting Started</h3><details open><summary>What is the Pokémon Reading Challenge?</summary><div class="faq-answer">The Pokémon Reading Challenge turns reading into a Pokémon-catching adventure. Each Pokémon has a reading prompt. Complete the prompt with a book, add that book, and mark the Pokémon as caught. Catch favorites, complete a region, challenge the Gyms, or try to catch them all — there is no required pace.</div></details><details><summary>Does the challenge have an end date?</summary><div class="faq-answer">No! This is an <strong>ongoing challenge with no end date</strong>. It has been running for about nine years; only the website is new. When a new year begins, the challenge continues with it. There is no December 31 deadline.</div></details><details><summary>What counts as a book?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Pretty much anything you consider reading can count: novels, novellas, short books, chapter books, picture books, graphic novels, manga, comics, audiobooks, fan fiction, and more. This challenge is about making reading fun, not policing what counts as a “real” book.</div></details><details><summary>Do DNFs count toward completing a Pokémon prompt?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>No.</strong> A DNF (did not finish) does not count as completing a Pokémon reading prompt or catching that Pokémon. Finish the book before using that reading for a catch.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use the same book for more than one Pokémon?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Normally, no. Each time you read a book, that reading can only count toward <strong>one Pokémon</strong>.<br><br>The exceptions are if you <strong>re-read the book</strong>, or when catching <strong>Unown</strong>. A re-read counts as a new reading, so you may use that new reading to catch a different Pokémon. <strong>For a re-read, BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong> Unown are a special exception: an Unown may reuse a book even if that same reading already counted for any other Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Example:</strong> If you read <em>Alice in Wonderland</em> and use it to catch Bulbasaur, you may still use that same reading to catch Unown A. You still could not use that reading to catch another regular Pokémon.<br><br>For the two special-character forms, <strong>Unown !</strong> requires a book title containing <strong>!</strong>, and <strong>Unown ?</strong> requires a book title containing <strong>?</strong>. Those forms still receive the normal Unown reuse exception.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use the same book again if I re-read it?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Yes.</strong> A re-read counts as a separate reading, so the same book can be used again for another Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Important: BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong><br><br>If both entries do not have dates, or if the dates are the same, the site will treat them as the same reading and will not allow the book to be reused.</div></details><details><summary>What’s the difference between my Display Name and Username?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Display Name</strong> is the name shown ar

<section id="faqPage" class="page"><div class="faq-shell"><div class="faq-hero"><div class="faq-kicker">READING FRENZY</div><h2>Pokémon Reading Challenge FAQ</h2><p>Everything you need to know about catching Pokémon, Safari and shiny hunting, achievements, Gym Battles, teams, Trainer Cards, backups, and the community.</p><div class="faq-evergreen"><strong>No deadline!</strong> The Pokémon Reading Challenge is ongoing and has no end date. It has been running for about nine years — the website is just the newest way to participate.</div></div><div id="recentUpdatesSection" class="faq-section recent-updates-section"><div class="recent-updates-heading"><div><div class="faq-kicker">WHAT'S NEW</div><h3>📣 Recent Updates</h3></div><button id="addSiteUpdateBtn" class="primary hidden" type="button" onclick="openSiteUpdateEditor()">+ Add Update</button></div><div id="siteUpdateEditor" class="site-update-editor hidden"><input id="siteUpdateTitle" maxlength="120" placeholder="Update title"><label for="siteUpdateDate">Date</label><input id="siteUpdateDate" type="date"><label for="siteUpdateBody">Description</label><textarea id="siteUpdateBody" maxlength="2000" rows="5" placeholder="Explain what changed..."></textarea><div class="site-update-editor-actions"><button class="primary" type="button" onclick="saveSiteUpdate()">Save Update</button><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="cancelSiteUpdateEditor()">Cancel</button></div></div><div id="siteUpdatesList" class="site-updates-list"><div class="muted">Loading recent updates…</div></div><button id="siteUpdatesViewMoreBtn" class="secondary site-updates-view-more hidden" type="button" onclick="toggleAllSiteUpdates()">View More Updates</button></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>📖 Getting Started</h3><details open><summary>What is the Pokémon Reading Challenge?</summary><div class="faq-answer">The Pokémon Reading Challenge turns reading into a Pokémon-catching adventure. Each Pokémon has a reading prompt. Complete the prompt with a book, add that book, and mark the Pokémon as caught. Catch favorites, complete a region, challenge the Gyms, or try to catch them all — there is no required pace.</div></details><details><summary>Does the challenge have an end date?</summary><div class="faq-answer">No! This is an <strong>ongoing challenge with no end date</strong>. It has been running for about nine years; only the website is new. When a new year begins, the challenge continues with it. There is no December 31 deadline.</div></details><details><summary>What counts as a book?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Pretty much anything you consider reading can count: novels, novellas, short books, chapter books, picture books, graphic novels, manga, comics, audiobooks, fan fiction, and more. This challenge is about making reading fun, not policing what counts as a “real” book.</div></details><details><summary>Do DNFs count toward completing a Pokémon prompt?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>No.</strong> A DNF (did not finish) does not count as completing a Pokémon reading prompt or catching that Pokémon. Finish the book before using that reading for a catch.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use the same book for more than one Pokémon?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Normally, no. Each time you read a book, that reading can only count toward <strong>one Pokémon</strong>.<br><br>The exceptions are if you <strong>re-read the book</strong>, or when catching <strong>Unown</strong>. A re-read counts as a new reading, so you may use that new reading to catch a different Pokémon. <strong>For a re-read, BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong> Unown are a special exception: an Unown may reuse a book even if that same reading already counted for any other Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Example:</strong> If you read <em>Alice in Wonderland</em> and use it to catch Bulbasaur, you may still use that same reading to catch Unown A. You still could not use that reading to catch another regular Pokémon.<br><br>For the two special-character forms, <strong>Unown !</strong> requires a book title containing <strong>!</strong>, and <strong>Unown ?</strong> requires a book title containing <strong>?</strong>. Those forms still receive the normal Unown reuse exception.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use the same book again if I re-read it?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Yes.</strong> A re-read counts as a separate reading, so the same book can be used again for another Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Important: BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong><br><br>If both entries do not have dates, or if the dates are the same, the site will treat them as the same reading and will not allow the book to be reused.</div></details><details><summary>What’s the difference between my Display Name and Username?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Display Name</strong> is the name shown around the site, including places like your profile and Trainer Card.<br><br><strong>Username</strong> is your unique account name.<br><br>You can set or update both under <strong>Settings</strong>. You are allowed to change your display name or username when needed, but please avoid changing your username too often so other readers and teammates can still recognize you. Usernames must remain unique and use 3–30 letters, numbers, or underscores.</div></details><details><summary>Is this an official Pokémon website?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>No.</strong> This is an unofficial, noncommercial fan-made reading challenge created by fans for fans. It is not affiliated with, endorsed by, sponsored by, or associated with The Pokémon Company International, Nintendo, Creatures Inc., or GAME FREAK inc.<br><br>Pokémon and all related names, characters, images, and trademarks are the property of their respective owners. No ownership of Pokémon intellectual 

===== TERM 'open' AT 2513029 =====
 class="faq-answer"><strong>Saved Shiny</strong> means you found a shiny but have not permanently caught it yet. <strong>Shiny Caught</strong> means the shiny has been caught and unlocked. The Pokédex has a separate filter for each list, and both lists show the shiny artwork.</div></details><details><summary>How do I shiny hunt a Pokémon I already caught?</summary><div class="faq-answer">In Safari, turn on <strong>All Pokémon — Caught + Uncaught</strong>. If a Pokémon you already caught rolls as shiny, choose <strong>Catch Shiny</strong>. You do <strong>not</strong> need to read another book or add another book because you already completed that Pokémon's regular catch.</div></details><details><summary>Does catching a shiny also catch the regular Pokémon?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Yes. Catching the shiny also counts the regular Pokémon as caught. The reverse is not true: catching the regular Pokémon does <strong>not</strong> automatically catch its shiny.</div></details><details><summary>What if I leave a shiny encounter without saving or catching it?</summary><div class="faq-answer">It is moved to the <strong>Missed Shinies</strong> dropdown in Safari. Select that Pokémon later to make the same shiny encounter appear again, then choose <strong>Save Shiny</strong> or <strong>Catch Shiny</strong>. Missed Shinies can hold up to <strong>4 unfinished shiny encounters</strong>. If all 4 spots are full and you find another shiny, Safari will make you save or catch that shiny before you can keep searching, so nothing gets deleted or replaced. Once a missed shiny is saved or caught, it leaves the Missed Shinies list.</div></details><details><summary>Can I switch between regular and shiny artwork?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Yes. After you permanently catch a shiny, open that Pokémon and use the <strong>Regular / ✨ Shiny</strong> toggle to choose which artwork you want to view. Your shiny remains caught either way.</div></details><details><summary>What are the shiny achievements?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Shiny Start</strong> — catch 1 shiny.<br><strong>Shiny Hunter</strong> — catch 3 shinies.<br><strong>Shiny Collector</strong> — catch 5 shinies.<br><strong>Shiny Specialist</strong> — catch 10 shinies.</div></details></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>🏆 Achievements & Binder</h3><details><summary>What is the Achievement Binder?</summary><div class="faq-answer">The Achievement Binder collects achievement cards you earn by reaching goals throughout the challenge. Achievements can come from catching Pokémon, collecting types, Safari encounters, shinies, Gym progress, teams, Trainer Teams, and completing regions.</div></details><details><summary>How do I earn an achievement card?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Complete the requirement shown on the locked card. When an achievement is ready, claim it to reveal the full card artwork in your binder.</div></details><details><summary>How do Type Collector achievements count dual-type Pokémon?</summary><div class="faq-answer">For Type Collector achievements, a Pokémon counts only toward its <strong>primary/first listed type</strong>. A dual-type Pokémon can still appear when browsing either type in the Pokédex, but it contributes to only its primary type's collector achievement.</div></details><details><summary>How does Safari Specialist work?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Safari Specialist</strong> requires 20 Pokémon that were marked as Safari encounters <strong>before</strong> they were caught. Once you catch a properly marked Safari Pokémon, that Safari credit stays attached to the catch.</div></details><details><summary>Can I download my achievement cards?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Yes. You can download an individual unlocked achievement card or use the binder download menu to download the full binder, locked cards, or unlocked cards.</div></details></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>🏟️ Gym Battles</h3><details open><summary>How do Gym Battles work?</summary><div class="faq-answer">When you battle a Gym Leader, you are completing the <strong>reading prompt for the Gym Pokémon you are trying to defeat</strong>.<br><br>For example, if the Gym Pokémon is <strong>Geodude</strong>, you are working with Geodude's reading prompt.<br><br>If you have <strong>already caught that same Pokémon</strong> in your Pokédex, you can choose <strong>Use Existing Caught Pokémon</strong>. That means you already completed that Pokémon's prompt before, so you can use your existing catch for the Gym Battle and you do <strong>not</strong> need to read another book.<br><br>If you <strong>have not caught that Pokémon</strong>, you will only have the <strong>New Entry</strong> option. With New Entry, read a new book that fits that Pokémon's prompt.<br><br>A New Entry can be used <strong>only for the Gym Battle</strong>, or you can choose to also count that book toward your Pokédex. If you count it toward the Pokédex, the same entry defeats the Gym Pokémon and catches that exact Pokémon for your Pokédex.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use a Gym-only book to catch a different Pokémon later?</summary><div class="faq-answer">No. If you choose to use a book strictly for the Gym Battle, that book counts only toward defeating that Gym Pokémon. It cannot later be reused to catch a different Pokémon in your Pokédex.</div></details><details><summary>Do I lose a Pokémon I use in a Gym Battle?</summary><div class="faq-answer">No. Using an already-caught Pokémon for a Gym Battle does not remove it from your Pokédex.</div></details><details><summary>Do Gym Battles count toward my team?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Yes. Gym completions contribute to the Gym Rankings for competitive teams.</div></details></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>⚔️ Teams & Community</h3><details><summary>Do I have to join a team?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Yes, every account must choose a team.</strong> You do not have to participate in competitiv

Team selection also helps the Reading Frenzy admin keep accounts and challenge participation organized, and it unlocks your team-themed Trainer Card and character choices.</div></details><details><summary>What are teams competing for?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Bragging rights and community fun! Team Stats tracks Pokémon caught and Gym progress for friendly competition.</div></details><details><summary>What are Team Pokémon Bonuses and bonus points?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Each team can have featured <strong>Team Pokémon Bonuses</strong>. Catching one of those featured Pokémon while it is active earns <strong>2 team points instead of the normal 1</strong> — your regular catch point plus one extra bonus point. For <strong>Poké Dolls</strong>, the same system awards <strong>2 community hearts instead of 1</strong>. Bonus credit only applies to qualifying catches made after the bonus feature became active; Pokémon that were already caught beforehand do not receive the extra point or heart.</div></details><details><summary>Should I choose a smaller team?</summary><div class="faq-answer">You can! Team selection shows current member totals. If you're torn between teams, joining a smaller one can help keep the competition balanced.</div></details><details><summary>What does a Team Leader do?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Team Leaders hype up their team, encourage members, and pass along issues, concerns, or questions. Team Leaders also have a private Discord chat.</div></details><details><summary>Where can I talk to my teammates?</summary><div class="faq-answer">The Reading Frenzy Discord has dedicated team chats for talking, sharing what you're catching, and showing off customized Trainer Cards.</div></details><details><summary>Is the team competition serious?</summary><div class="faq-answer">No. Friendly competition and playful trash talk are fine, but please be respectful. The point is to read, catch Pokémon, and have fun together.</div></details><details><summary>Can I change

Your Pokédex, books, caught Pokémon, Gym progress, Trainer Card, and personal challenge progress belong to your account. Team Stats combines certain totals for competition without combining personal collections.</div></details><details><summary>What information can other members see?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Community features may show challenge-related information such as your username and team affiliation. Your teammates can see your username in My Team Members. Your email is not displayed there.</div></details></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>🎉 Events & Updates</h3><details><summary>Will there be special events?</summary><div class="faq-answer">There may be holiday, seasonal, or other special events throughout the challenge, so keep an eye out.</div></details><details><summary>Is the website finished?</summary><div class="faq-answer">The core challenge is live, but the website will continue receiving updates, improvements, bug fixes, and potentially new features.</div></details></div><div class="faq-section faq-community"><h3>💬 Help & Community</h3><p>The Reading Frenzy Discord is the main hub for challenge discussion, team chats, help, bug reports, suggestions, and sharing progress.</p><a class="faq-discord-button" href="https://discord.gg/6UMU3aj9he" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Join the Reading Frenzy Discord</a><details><summary>I found a bug or need help. });function entry(id){
let e=challengeData[id];
if(!e||typeof e!=="object"||Array.isArray(e))e={};
if(typeof e.prompt!=="string")e.prompt="";
if(typeof e.notes!=="string")e.notes="";
if(!Array.isArray(e.books))e.books=[];
if(typeof e.caught!=="boolean")e.caught=!!e.caught;
if(typeof e.wishlist!=="boolean")e.wishlist=!!e.wishlist;
if(typeof e.plannedBook!=="string")e.plannedBook=String(e.plannedBook||"");
if(typeof e.currentlyCatching!=="boolean")e.currentlyCatching=!!e.currentlyCatching;
if(typeof e.safariMarked!=="boolean")e.safariMarked=!!e.safariMarked;
if(typeof e.safariCatch!=="boolean")e.safariCatch=!!e.safariCatch;
if(typeof e.safariMarkedAt!=="string")e.safariMarkedAt=String(e.safariMarkedAt||"");
if(typeof e.shinySaved!=="boolean")e.shinySaved=!!e.shinySaved;
if(typeof e.shinySavedAt!=="string")e.shinySavedAt=String(e.shinySavedAt||"");
if(typeof e.shinyCaught!=="boolean")e.shinyCaught=!!e.shinyCaught;
if(e.shinyView!=="shiny"&&e.shinyView!=="regular")e.shinyView=e.shinyCaught?"shiny":"regular";
if(e.caught)e.currentlyCatching=false;
challengeData[id]=e;
return e;
}const caught=p=>!!challengeData[p.id]?.caught;
const wishlisted=p=>!!challengeData[p.id]?.wishlist;
const currentlyCatching=p=>!!challengeData[p.id]?.currentlyCatching&&!caught(p);

}

async function loadAchievementTeamTagData(){
  if(!user||!Array.isArray(factions)||!factions.length)return;
  if(achievementTeamBonusLoadPromise)return achievementTeamBonusLoadPromise;
  achievementTeamBonusLoadPromise=(async()=>{
    const bySlug={};
    const results=await Promise.all(factions.map(async f=>{
      try{
        const{data,error}=await sb.rpc("get_team_page_preview",{p_faction_id:f.id});
        return error?null:{f,data};
      }catch{return null}
    }));
    results.filter(Boolean).forEach(({f,data})=>{
      const rows=Array.isArray(data?.bonuses)?data.bonuses:[];
      rows.forEach(b=>{
        const slug=String(b.catalog_slug||"").toLowerCase();
        if(!slug)return;
        (bySlug[slug]||(bySlug[slug]=[])).push({slug:String(f.slug||""),name:String(f.name||"Team")});
      });
    });
    achievementTeamBonusTagsBySlug=bySlug;
    populateAchievementTeamFilterOptions();
    syncAchievementMilestoneNotification();
    if(document.getElementById("achievementsPage")?.classList.contains("active"))renderAchievementShell();
    if(document.getElementById("dexPage")?.classList.contains("active"))renderDex();
    if(document.getElementById("detailPage")?.classList.contains("active")&&currentPokemonId)openPokemon(currentPokemonId,true);
  })().finally(()=>achievementTeamBonusLoadPromise=null);
  return achievementTeamBonusLoadPromise;
}
function syncDexAchievementFilterVisibility(){
  const wrap=document.getElementById("dexAchievementFilters");
  if(wrap)wrap.classList.remove("hidden");
}

It has been running for about nine years — the website is just the newest way to participate.</div></div><div id="recentUpdatesSection" class="faq-section recent-updates-section"><div class="recent-updates-heading"><div><div class="faq-kicker">WHAT'S NEW</div><h3>📣 Recent Updates</h3></div><button id="addSiteUpdateBtn" class="primary hidden" type="button" onclick="openSiteUpdateEditor()">+ Add Update</button></div><div id="siteUpdateEditor" class="site-update-editor hidden"><input id="siteUpdateTitle" maxlength="120" placeholder="Update title"><label for="siteUpdateDate">Date</label><input id="siteUpdateDate" type="date"><label for="siteUpdateBody">Description</label><textarea id="siteUpdateBody" maxlength="2000" rows="5" placeholder="Explain what changed..."></textarea><div class="site-update-editor-actions"><button class="primary" type="button" onclick="saveSiteUpdate()">Save Update</button><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="cancelSiteUpdateEditor()">Cancel</button></div></div><div id="siteUpdatesList" class="site-updates-list"><div class="muted">Loading recent updates…</div></div><button id="siteUpdatesViewMoreBtn" class="secondary site-updates-view-more hidden" type="button" onclick="toggleAllSiteUpdates()">View More Updates</button></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>📖 Getting Started</h3><details open><summary>What is the Pokémon Reading Challenge?</summary><div class="faq-answer">The Pokémon Reading Challenge turns reading into a Pokémon-catching adventure. Each Pokémon has a reading prompt. Complete the prompt with a book, add that book, and mark the Pokémon as caught. Catch favorites, complete a region, challenge the Gyms, or try to catch them all — there is no required pace.</div></details><details><summary>Does the challenge have an end date?</summary><div class="faq-answer">No! This is an <strong>ongoing challenge with no end date</strong>. It has been running for about nine years; only the website is new. When a new year begins, the challenge continues with it. There is no December 31 deadline.</div></details><details><summary>What counts as a book?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Pretty much anything you consider reading can count: novels, novellas, short books, chapter books, picture books, graphic novels, manga, comics, audiobooks, fan fiction, and more. This challenge is about making reading fun, not policing what counts as a “real” book.</div></details><details><summary>Do DNFs count toward completing a Pokémon prompt?</summary><div class="faq-a
