<section id="faqPage" class="page"><div class="faq-shell"><div class="faq-hero"><div class="faq-kicker">READING FRENZY</div><h2>Pokémon Reading Challenge FAQ</h2><p>Everything you need to know about catching Pokémon, Safari and shiny hunting, achievements, Gym Battles, teams, Trainer Cards, backups, and the community.</p><div class="faq-evergreen"><strong>No deadline!</strong> The Pokémon Reading Challenge is ongoing and has no end date. It has been running for about nine years — the website is just the newest way to participate.</div></div><div id="recentUpdatesSection" class="faq-section recent-updates-section"><div class="recent-updates-heading"><div><div class="faq-kicker">WHAT'S NEW</div><h3>📣 Recent Updates</h3></div><button id="addSiteUpdateBtn" class="primary hidden" type="button" onclick="openSiteUpdateEditor()">+ Add Update</button></div><div id="siteUpdateEditor" class="site-update-editor hidden"><input id="siteUpdateTitle" maxlength="120" placeholder="Update title"><label for="siteUpdateDate">Date</label><input id="siteUpdateDate" type="date"><label for="siteUpdateBody">Description</label><textarea id="siteUpdateBody" maxlength="2000" rows="5" placeholder="Explain what changed..."></textarea><div class="site-update-editor-actions"><button class="primary" type="button" onclick="saveSiteUpdate()">Save Update</button><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="cancelSiteUpdateEditor()">Cancel</button></div></div><div id="siteUpdatesList" class="site-updates-list"><div class="muted">Loading recent updates…</div></div><button id="siteUpdatesViewMoreBtn" class="secondary site-updates-view-more hidden" type="button" onclick="toggleAllSiteUpdates()">View More Updates</button></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>📖 Getting Started</h3><details open><summary>What is the Pokémon Reading Challenge?</summary><div class="faq-answer">The Pokémon Reading Challenge turns reading into a Pokémon-catching adventure. Each Pokémon has a reading prompt. Complete the prompt with a book, add that book, and mark the Pokémon as caught. Catch favorites, complete a region, challenge the Gyms, or try to catch them all — there is no required pace.</div></details><details><summary>Does the challenge have an end date?</summary><div class="faq-answer">No! This is an <strong>ongoing challenge with no end date</strong>. It has been running for about nine years; only the website is new. When a new year begins, the challenge continues with it. There is no December 31 deadline.</div></details><details><summary>What counts as a book?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Pretty much anything you consider reading can count: novels, novellas, short books, chapter books, picture books, graphic novels, manga, comics, audiobooks, fan fiction, and more. This challenge is about making reading fun, not policing what counts as a “real” book.</div></details><details><summary>Do DNFs count toward completing a Pokémon prompt?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>No.</strong> A DNF (did not finish) does not count as completing a Pokémon reading prompt or catching that Pokémon. Finish the book before using that reading for a catch.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use the same book for more than one Pokémon?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Normally, no. Each time you read a book, that reading can only count toward <strong>one Pokémon</strong>.<br><br>The exceptions are if you <strong>re-read the book</strong>, or when catching <strong>Unown</strong>. A re-read counts as a new reading, so you may use that new reading to catch a different Pokémon. <strong>For a re-read, BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong> Unown are a special exception: an Unown may reuse a book even if that same reading already counted for any other Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Example:</strong> If you read <em>Alice in Wonderland</em> and use it to catch Bulbasaur, you may still use that same reading to catch Unown A. You still could not use that reading to catch another regular Pokémon.<br><br>For the two special-character forms, <strong>Unown !</strong> requires a book title containing <strong>!</strong>, and <strong>Unown ?</strong> requires a book title containing <strong>?</strong>. Those forms still receive the normal Unown reuse exception.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use the same book again if I re-read it?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Yes.</strong> A re-read counts as a separate reading, so the same book can be used again for another Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Important: BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong><br><br>If both entries do not have dates, or if the dates are the same, the site will treat them as the same reading and will not allow the book to be reused.</div></details><details><summary>What’s the difference between my Display Name and Username?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Display Name</strong> is the name shown around the site, including places like your profile and Trainer Card.<br><br><strong>Username</strong> is your unique account name.<br><br>You can set or update both under <strong>Settings</strong>. You are allowed to change your display name or username when needed, but please avoid changing your username too often so other readers and teammates can still recognize you. Usernames must remain unique and use 3–30 letters, numbers, or underscores.</div></details><details><summary>Is this an official Pokémon website?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>No.</strong> This is an unofficial, noncommercial fan-made reading challenge created by fans for fans. It is not affiliated with, endorsed by, sponsored by, or associated with The Pokémon Company International, Nintendo, Creatures Inc., or GAME FREAK inc.<br><br>Pokémon and all related names, characters, images, and trademarks are the property of their respective owners. Those forms still receive the normal Unown reuse exception.</div></details><details><summary>Can I use the same book again if I re-read it?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Yes.</strong> A re-read counts as a separate reading, so the same book can be used again for another Pokémon.<br><br><strong>Important: BOTH book entries must have a read date entered, and the two read dates must be different.</strong><br><br>If both entries do not have dates, or if the dates are the same, the site will treat them as the same reading and will not allow the book to be reused.</div></details><details><summary>What’s the difference between my Display Name and Username?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>Display Name</strong> is the name shown around the site, including places like your profile and Trainer Card.<br><br><strong>Username</strong> is your unique account name.<br><br>You can set or update both under <strong>Settings</strong>. You are allowed to change your display name or username when needed, but please avoid changing your username too often so other readers and teammates can still recognize you. Usernames must remain unique and use 3–30 letters, numbers, or underscores.</div></details><details><summary>Is this an official Pokémon website?</summary><div class="faq-answer"><strong>No.</strong> This is an unofficial, noncommercial fan-made reading challenge created by fans for fans. It is not affiliated with, endorsed by, sponsored by, or associated with The Pokémon Company International, Nintendo, Creatures Inc., or GAME FREAK inc.<br><br>Pokémon and all related names, characters, images, and trademarks are the property of their respective owners. No ownership of Pokémon intellectual property is claimed.</div></details><details><summary>Can I add books I read before joining the website?</summary><div class="faq-answer">Yes! Since the website launched in 2026, you can backlog books you read during <strong>2026</strong>, including books finished before creating your account. Each book can still only be used once.</div></details><details><summary>What if I'm not sure my book fits a prompt?</summary><div class="faq-answer">There is <strong>no prompt policing</strong>. If you genuinely think your book fits the prompt, use it. Prompts are here to make choosing books fun, not stressful.</div></details></div><div class="faq-section"><h3>🔎 Pokédex & Catching Pokémon</h3><details><summary>How do I catch a Pokémon?<
